903 entries, autopsy-agent to azure-verified-modules-terraform. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **autopsy-agent**: Use when a subagent should score codebase health per module (0-100 across 6 dimensions) and rank the worst tech debt, for a rescue workflow or project diagnosis. - **autoqec-ideator**: Research-round Ideator for AutoQEC. Proposes the next predecoder hypothesis given env_spec, current Pareto front, recent history, and machine_state. Output is a single fenced JSON block matching docs/contracts/interfaces.md §2.5. ([qualit527/qec-ai-decoder](https://github.com/qualit527/qec-ai-decoder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoqec-ideator.md) · ★3)
- **autor**: Schreibt, überarbeitet oder kürzt ein Kapitel oder Unterkapitel (eine Kapiteldatei) nach Plan und Stilregeln, prüft mit stil.mjs. Bei /schreiben und zum Umsetzen von Prüfergebnissen. ([koljaschoepe/scientific-writing](https://github.com/koljaschoepe/scientific-writing/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autor.md) · ★13)
- **autor**: Convierte una solicitud aprobable en cuatro archivos de especificación MicroSDD; no implementa código. ([lgzarturo/microsdd](https://github.com/lgzarturo/microsdd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autor.md))
- **autor-de-testes**: Escreve testes para o código novo da branch no controle-financeiro (xUnit + AutoFixture + FluentAssertions). Use depois de implementar e antes do PR, ou quando o diff traz regra de domínio, importador de extrato, ferramenta MCP ou lógica do agente sem teste. Feature sem teste não está pronta. ([humbertosampaio/controle-financeiro](https://github.com/humbertosampaio/controle-financeiro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autor-de-testes.md))
- **autor-regresiones**: Autor independiente de pruebas de regresión de Prosecnur. Usar tras un diagnóstico o ante cambios de alto riesgo para crear la prueba mínima que falle por el defecto y pase con la reparación. Solo modifica tests, helpers de test y fixtures deliberadas. ([GonzaloAlmendariz/prosecnur-app](https://github.com/GonzaloAlmendariz/prosecnur-app/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autor-regresiones.md))
- **autor-testes**: Escreve os testes de uma tarefa do tasks.md ANTES da implementação, a partir dos critérios de aceite da spec. Use no início de cada tarefa. Entrega testes que compilam e falham pelo motivo certo. ([alebarre/cuidaMaisAplicativo](https://github.com/alebarre/cuidaMaisAplicativo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autor-testes.md))
- **autoreason**: AutoReason (v2027.1.x+, early January 2027) is an open-source, autonomous reasoning framework and execution engine developed by Nous Research. Designed to enable large language models (LLMs) to perform complex, multi-step logical reasoning, automated hypothesis testing, and continuous code debugging with minimal human guidance, AutoReason introduces production-grade "Reasoning-as-a-Service" (RaaS) ([joanmarcriera/Home-office-automations](https://github.com/joanmarcriera/Home-office-automations/blob/HEAD/docs/tools/agents/autoreason.md) · ★7)
- **autoreason-judge**: Blind tournament judge for A/B/AB comparisons on risky changes. Fresh context every call. Ranks three candidates (incumbent A / adversarial B / synthesis AB) via Borda count. "Do nothing" is a first-class outcome — explicitly preferred when differences are stylistic. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/agents/autoreason-judge.md))
- **autorepair.py**: &gt; **Essential Process**: &gt; Real-Time Fleet Incident Subscriber & Capability-Aware Tiered Auto-Repair Coordinator. Listens on brain-partitioned NATS subjects emitted by watchdog-agent, enriches incidents with correlated TimescaleDB history & source snippets, classifies severity Tiers 1-4 per Prompt-Fleet-AutoRepair.md, and gates mutations behind isolated /tmp git worktree tests and 3-strike circuit ([Bastien-Antigravity/obsidian-brain](https://github.com/Bastien-Antigravity/obsidian-brain/blob/HEAD/11-Code-Doc/08-Squad-Agent/obsidian-brain/08-Squad-Agent/src/agents/autorepair.py.md) · 2 copies)
- **autoresearch**: ../../generated-examples/autoresearch.md ([skypilot-org/skypilot](https://github.com/skypilot-org/skypilot/blob/HEAD/docs/source/examples/agents/autoresearch.md) · ★11k · 3 copies)
- **autoresearch**: Runs Agentic SEO Autoresearch experiment loops for skills, fixtures, regressions, and keep/reject decisions. ([conversion-agency/agentic-seo-skills](https://github.com/conversion-agency/agentic-seo-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch.md) · ★52 · 2 copies)
- **autoresearch**: Autoresearch loop agent — the single managing agent that runs the open-ended improvement loop for any phase. Makes one focused change per iteration and judges it itself (no worker/evaluator fan-out), keeping or reverting against the current best. Its memory is a per-loop wiki it reads before and writes after every move. Never stops on its own judgement; always stops at the caps set at setup, on a human stop, or after repeated tool errors. Inspired by Andrej Karpathy's autoresearch (MIT). ([stanislavjiricek/neuroflow](https://github.com/stanislavjiricek/neuroflow/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch.md) · ★8)
- **autoresearch**: Overnight skill optimizer. Runs N iterations of the Karpathy autoresearch loop: generate variant → eval against binary test cases → commit if better → discard if not. Use to improve ralph, tester, architect, or any skill. Makes ~12-15 experiments/hour. Run with --allow-all --max-autopilot-continues 100 for overnight sessions. Triggered by: 'autoresearch', 'improve skill overnight', 'optimize skill', 'run experiments'. ([elihuvillaraus/skills](https://github.com/elihuvillaraus/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch.agent.md) · ★3)
- **AutoResearch**: Autonomous quantitative strategy researcher. Runs experiment loops: modify config → backtest → evaluate → keep/discard → repeat. Inspired by karpathy/autoresearch, adapted for trading strategy discovery. ([louisleech10/quantitative_trading_system](https://github.com/louisleech10/quantitative_trading_system/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/autoresearch.agent.md) · ★2)
- **autoresearch**: Autonomous experiment worker — runs a batch of autoresearch experiments, then self-terminates ([sergical/pi-config](https://github.com/sergical/pi-config/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch.md) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **autoresearch-agent**: Automated ML experiment optimization using tree search — designs experiments, generates code, evaluates results, and iterates ([Sansar35/claude-agent](https://github.com/Sansar35/claude-agent/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-agent.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **autoresearch-agent-improver**: Improve a target agent.md with a Karpathy-style AutoResearch keep-or-revert loop. ([florianbuetow/kamino451](https://github.com/florianbuetow/kamino451/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearch-agent-improver.md) · ★8)
- **autoresearch-analyst**: Self-improvement agent for retrospectives, improvements, definition reviews, and full improve-validate cycles. Usable standalone or by orchestration. ([bmjcoding/agent-toolkit](https://github.com/bmjcoding/agent-toolkit/blob/HEAD/claude-code/agents/autoresearch-analyst.md) · 2 copies)
- **autoresearch-code-subagent**: Autonomous code optimization loop — modify, verify by benchmark, keep/revert against any metric (coverage, bundle size, runtime, errors). Full repo edit. ([darellchua2/civiltekk-skills](https://github.com/darellchua2/civiltekk-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-code-subagent.md) · ★7)
- **autoresearch-driver**: Per-iteration profile mutator for the autoresearch loop. Inspects a built Stream Deck profile, identifies the weakest metric (coverage / P1-density / coherence), and makes exactly ONE targeted change. Uses the opendeck MCP server + ProfileEditor. Invoked by the autoresearch skill — not directly by the user. ([thisis-romar/opendeck-factory](https://github.com/thisis-romar/opendeck-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearch-driver.md) · ★7)
- **autoresearch-eval-agent**: Eval Agent for AutoResearch. Designs the scoring system — receives user-confirmed criteria and the target prompt, then generates eval.py + test_cases.json (deterministic mode) or rubric.md + test_cases.json (AI judge mode). The main agent never sees the eval artifacts in detail. ([BenAIOS/benai-skills](https://github.com/BenAIOS/benai-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-eval-agent.md) · ★85 · 11 copies)
- **autoresearch-eval-author**: Design the immutable AutoResearch eval.py harness and task schema for a target agent optimization problem. ([florianbuetow/kamino451](https://github.com/florianbuetow/kamino451/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearch-eval-author.md) · ★8)
- **autoresearch-finalize**: Finalize an autoresearch session into clean, reviewable branches. Use when asked to "finalize autoresearch", "clean up experiments", "split the autoresearch branch", or "prepare autoresearch for review". Runs in isolation and drives scripts/finalize.sh. ([seanGSISG/cc-autoresearch](https://github.com/seanGSISG/cc-autoresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-finalize.md))
- **autoresearch-judge**: Judge Agent for AutoResearch. Scores outputs against a locked rubric for quality assessment. Operates with fresh context every iteration — knows NOTHING about iteration count, prompt changes, or optimization goals. Only follows the rubric. ([BenAIOS/benai-skills](https://github.com/BenAIOS/benai-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-judge.md) · ★85 · 11 copies)
- **autoresearch-llm-evaluator**: Semantically judge AutoResearch task trajectories and produce structured failure-mode analysis. ([florianbuetow/kamino451](https://github.com/florianbuetow/kamino451/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearch-llm-evaluator.md) · ★8)
- **autoresearch-ml-subagent**: Autonomous ML training research loop — modify train.py, train, parse val_bpb, keep/revert. NVIDIA GPU required; path-restricted edits (train.py + research files). ([darellchua2/civiltekk-skills](https://github.com/darellchua2/civiltekk-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-ml-subagent.md) · ★7)
- **autoresearch-orchestrator**: Use this agent to run an autoresearch experiment session end-to-end — setup, baseline, and a batch of edit→measure→keep/discard experiments — and return a structured checkpoint. Typical triggers include the /run-autoresearch command dispatching a new optimization goal, resuming an existing session found in autoresearch.jsonl, and continuing a session after a previous batch checkpoint. Not for one-off benchmarks or status-only questions (answer those directly from autoresearch.jsonl). See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([proyecto26/autoresearch-ai-plugin](https://github.com/proyecto26/autoresearch-ai-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-orchestrator.md) · ★12)
- **autoresearch-program-author**: Generate a task-specific program.md for AutoResearch prompt optimization. ([florianbuetow/kamino451](https://github.com/florianbuetow/kamino451/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearch-program-author.md) · ★8)
- **autoresearch-research-subagent**: Autonomous literature-review loop (Tier 2, web-only) — fetches papers, structured summaries, living research.md with category tracking. No Bash; research-files-only edits. ([darellchua2/civiltekk-skills](https://github.com/darellchua2/civiltekk-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-research-subagent.md) · ★7)
- **autoresearch-runner**: You are the autoresearch-runner agent. You execute autonomous experiment loops on GPU instances. ([sublimotion/agent-aiops-on-aws](https://github.com/sublimotion/agent-aiops-on-aws/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearch-runner.md) · ★4)
- **autoresearch-runner**: Autonomous keep/discard loop — edits a target file, measures a metric each iteration, keeps improvements and resets failures. Runs until budget exhausted, 3 consecutive crashes, or user interrupt. ([cardinalconseils/claude-starter](https://github.com/cardinalconseils/claude-starter/blob/HEAD/legacy/agents/autoresearch-runner.md) · ★3)
- **autoresearch-runner**: Executes the autoresearch optimization loop. Generates hypotheses, makes atomic changes within scope, measures metrics, keeps improvements or reverts regressions. Runs autonomously until convergence or budget exhaustion. ([tyroneross/RossLabs-AI-Toolkit](https://github.com/tyroneross/RossLabs-AI-Toolkit/blob/HEAD/archive/build-loop-auto-research/agents/autoresearch-runner.md))
- **autoresearch-setup**: Interactive setup agent — produces autoresearch.md + autoresearch.sh + optional autoresearch.checks.sh from a natural-language objective so the loop can run. ([jgmontoya/shaka](https://github.com/jgmontoya/shaka/blob/HEAD/defaults/system/agents/autoresearch-setup.md) · ★28)
- **autoresearch-template**: TEMPLATE — copy this to ~/.claude/agents/autoresearch-&lt;your-project&gt;.md and fill in the {{PLACEHOLDERS}} before use. Autonomous ML/empirical researcher: reads research/state.jsonl, checks compute (cluster scheduler + local GPU), launches the next highest-priority experiment, logs results with the project's schema, grounds new hypotheses against prior literature, and iterates toward publishable findings. Self-paces via ScheduleWakeup. ([GonzaloFuentes1/auto-lab](https://github.com/GonzaloFuentes1/auto-lab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearch-template.md))
- **autoresearch-test-runner**: Test Runner Agent for AutoResearch. Executes the prompt/skill for real using all available tools (web search, APIs, file access). Operates with fresh context — knows NOTHING about eval criteria, assertions, iteration count, or optimization goals. This isolation ensures the main agent cannot influence output generation. ([BenAIOS/benai-skills](https://github.com/BenAIOS/benai-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/autoresearch-test-runner.md) · ★85 · 11 copies)
- **autoresearcher**: One-bounded-dig research experimenter — reads the brief, proposes ONE hypothesis targeting ONE harness component, fetches ≤5 sources, edits ONE section, returns a scored diff. Karpathy-autoresearch pattern. ([frankxai/agentic-creator-os](https://github.com/frankxai/agentic-creator-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autoresearcher.md) · ★12 · 2 copies)
- **autoreview**: Use this subagent to run a closeout structured code review after non-trivial edits, before commit/ship, or to review a local branch/PR/commit. It is read-only, verifies every finding against real code, returns a JSON findings bundle against a fixed schema, and rejects speculative/broad/unrealistic findings. Example — user finished a non-trivial change and wants a closeout check ("rode um autoreview antes de commitar"); the assistant uses the autoreview subagent to review the change bundle. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/blob/HEAD/autoreview-agnostic/claude-code/.claude/agents/autoreview.md))
- **autorun-implementer**: Implementer for a jnext autonomous run — carries out a planner's plan (or a team-run unit spec) on its branch, gates it, reports. Dispatched by the main-session orchestrator only (autonomous-run or team-run). ([jorgegv/jnext](https://github.com/jorgegv/jnext/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autorun-implementer.md) · ★55)
- **autorun-plan-orchestrator**: Use when executing an approved AUTORUN or overnight unattended multi-wave plan; when the operator wants autonomous execution with heartbeat, AUTORUN-STATE, right-fit named agents, park-the-branch-continue-the-rest, and the tests → adversary → `/ponytail-review` → optional external-LLM → re-test → merge gate stack. Not for writing the plan (use deep-plan-swarm / plan-synthesizer). Not for a single mechanical edit (use exec-mechanical). ([LCS-Adam/standing-orders](https://github.com/LCS-Adam/standing-orders/blob/HEAD/agents/autorun-plan-orchestrator.md) · ★1)
- **autorun-planner**: Planner for a jnext autonomous run — diagnoses one GitHub issue (or one team-run unit) to its root cause and writes the plan the implementer follows. Does not write the fix. Dispatched by the main-session orchestrator only (autonomous-run or team-run). ([jorgegv/jnext](https://github.com/jorgegv/jnext/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autorun-planner.md) · ★55)
- **autorun-reviewer**: Independent reviewer for a jnext autonomous run — reviews an issue or team-run unit branch against its plan and gives a binary APPROVE/REJECT. Never reviews its own work. Dispatched by the main-session orchestrator only (autonomous-run or team-run). ([jorgegv/jnext](https://github.com/jorgegv/jnext/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/autorun-reviewer.md) · ★55)

Never stops until the selected run reaches a real terminal condition. ([Calibrax-ai/autoship](https://github.com/Calibrax-ai/autoship/blob/HEAD/packages/core/.claude/agents/autoship-controller.md))

- **autospec-checkpoint**: Writes coherent checkpoint summaries from journal entries. Produces both full and OK-only checkpoints. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-checkpoint.md))
- **autospec-compartmentalizer**: Analyzes a formal spec and splits it into compartments with identified intersections. Follows the compartmentalization algorithm. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-compartmentalizer.md))
- **autospec-judge**: Impartial judge for spec improvement proposals. Rules on proposer/reviewer disagreements with spec-level evidence. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-judge.md))
- **autospec-novelty**: Verifies whether a candidate-novel technique is genuinely new. Two-tier: sonnet for prior work search, opus only if no prior work found. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-novelty.md))
- **autospec-novelty-deep**: Deep novelty verification (stages 2-3). Only launched when sonnet novelty checker found no prior work. Handles generality check and adversarial verification. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-novelty-deep.md))
- **autospec-proposer**: Proposes structural improvements to a formal spec compartment. Draws from techniques registry and may discover novel techniques. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-proposer.md))
- **autospec-reviewer**: Adversarial reviewer of spec improvement proposals. Attacks claims, finds counterexamples, checks property preservation. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-reviewer.md))
- **autospec-seeder**: Seeds the techniques registry for a spec by loading universal techniques, domain files, and optionally searching for additional domain-specific ones. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-seeder.md))
- **autospec-writer**: Mechanical file I/O agent. Assembles triad outputs into journal schema, maintains status.json and registry.json for live dashboard consumption. Supports batched operations. ([heytdep/autospec](https://github.com/heytdep/autospec/blob/HEAD/agents/autospec-writer.md))
- **autotask**: Backend-triggered agent for the board's "Generate Tasks" AI action. Given a natural-language prompt, it produces a list of Kanban tasks (title, type, description, optional pipeline) as JSON. Invoked by the server (src/handlers/autoTask.js), not launched interactively. ([oscarmenendezgarcia/prism](https://github.com/oscarmenendezgarcia/prism/blob/HEAD/agents/autotask.md) · ★8)
- **autotel-instrumentor**: Add autotel instrumentation to uninstrumented code. Uses trace(), span(), getRequestLogger(), createStructuredError(), and track() to add spans, request-scoped context, structured errors, and product events. Knows all framework integrations (Hono, Fastify, TanStack Start, Cloudflare, Next.js, Express, MCP, edge). Use PROACTIVELY when writing new handlers, services, or API routes. ([jagreehal/autotel](https://github.com/jagreehal/autotel/blob/HEAD/autotel-observability/agents/autotel-instrumentor.md) · ★8)
- **autotel-reviewer**: Review code for autotel instrumentation quality. Checks for missing spans, scattered console.log, generic Error throws, missing request logger, incorrect init patterns, and anti-patterns. Use when reviewing PRs or auditing existing code that uses or should use autotel. ([jagreehal/autotel](https://github.com/jagreehal/autotel/blob/HEAD/autotel-observability/agents/autotel-reviewer.md) · ★8)
- **autotest**: E2E test developer for the CRM (Playwright @crm/e2e). 4 modes: post-coder spec / docs-driven / task-driven / fix-flaky (same-day SLA, contracts §3.3). Dispatch decision D3: if the Reviewer suggests a test fix — decides who handles it (AutoTest vs Coder) per docs/architecture/2026-05-23-dev-flow-rca.md. Delega qui per «un rider ci fa causa / ricorso INL / rischio riqualificazione in giudizio / giurisprudenza rider / ci convocano in tribunale / quanto rischiamo su questa causa». (→ buste paga/inquadramento operativo = **consulente-lavoro**; sicurezza lavoro = **rspp**) ([NicolaeRotaru/ad-mycity](https://github.com/NicolaeRotaru/ad-mycity/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/avvocato-lavoro.md))

- **awareness.py**: Awareness 是 Agent 的自我认知配置——它知道自己是谁、有什么能力、适用于哪些场景。这些信息存储在 `instance_awareness` 表里，通过 `AwarenessModule` 的实例 ID 关联到 Agent。这个路由文件暴露 GET/PUT 两个接口，让前端能读取和编辑 Awareness 内容。 ([NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus](https://github.com/NetMindAI-Open/NarraNexus/blob/HEAD/.mindflow/mirror/backend/routes/agents/awareness.py.md) · ★91)

- **AWARENESS_MODULE_SEEDS**: Starting points for each module writer, from the orchestrator's planning. Read `AWARENESS_COURSE_BRIEF.md` first. These seeds are suggestions: keep what works, change what doesn't, and say why in your report. Scope and boundaries are firmer than the game ideas. ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AWARENESS_MODULE_SEEDS.md) · ★6)
- **AWARENESS_REVIEW_BRIEF**: For reviewers of the module plans in `planning_notes/security_awareness_course/modules/`. Read `docs/agents/AWARENESS_COURSE_BRIEF.md` first: it sets the kind of game, the quality bar and the writing rules the writers worked to. You are **read-only**: don't edit any file. Report findings; the orchestrator sends them back to the writers. ([cliffe/BreakEscape](https://github.com/cliffe/BreakEscape/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/AWARENESS_REVIEW_BRIEF.md) · ★6)
- **away-answerer**: Read-only answerer for Away mode. It reads one question round from a Claude Code session that asked the user something, picks an option label for every question or holds the round, and reports through its final message only, ending with one `AWAY-ANSWER: &lt;json&gt;` line. It never edits, never writes, never posts anywhere. ([masnun-siam/ai-workflow](https://github.com/masnun-siam/ai-workflow/blob/HEAD/prompts/agents/away-answerer.md) · ★4)
- **away-summary**: When the user has been silent for a configurable threshold (default 4 hours), the next inbound message triggers a short markdown digest that summarises everything the agent did during the silence: goals completed, aborts, failures, and turn counts. Default is **disabled** — per-binding opt-in. ([lordmacu/nexo-rs](https://github.com/lordmacu/nexo-rs/blob/HEAD/docs/src/agents/away-summary.md) · ★9)
- **away-team**: The Away Team orchestrator. Runs only as the main thread the user selected (Claude Code: claude --agent away-team or /away-team; Copilot: /agent, away-team), never as a subagent. Do not delegate to it. It beams down away-team:away-team-mapper, away-team:away-team-investigator, away-team:away-team-basher, away-team:away-team-pr-writer and away-team:away-team-reviewer, stops at gates to ask the user, and relays their reports. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/agents/away-team.md))
- **away-team**: The Away Team orchestrator. Runs only as the main thread the user selected (Claude Code: claude --agent away-team or /away-team; Copilot: /agent, away-team), never as a subagent. Do not delegate to it. It beams down away-team-mapper, away-team-investigator, away-team-basher, away-team-pr-writer and away-team-reviewer, stops at gates to ask the user, and relays their reports. - **away-team-basher**: Fixes a bug from an investigator Diagnosis, or does a small fully-specified change. Failing test first, minimal root-cause fix at the point all callers share, run tests, commit, report evidence or a Blocked report. Does not push or open PRs. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/agents/away-team-basher.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-investigator**: Root-causes a bug quickly, or gives a second opinion on a proposal already on an issue. Read-only. Reproduces, localises, tests one hypothesis at a time, returns a Diagnosis with path:line evidence and a fix recommendation, a Review of what the item and its comments propose, or a Blocked report. Never edits files. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/agents/away-team-investigator.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-mapper**: Trawls a solution and writes docs/CODEMAP.md (entry points, module boundaries, data flow, invariants, verified build/test commands) so investigator and basher can navigate without re-reading the repo. Run once per repo, then refresh. Returns a Map report or a Blocked report. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/copilot/agents/away-team-mapper.agent.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-pr-writer**: Opens or refreshes a pull request from the current branch in the house format. Conventional-commit title, TL;DR body, full technical breakdown posted as PR review comments. Uses the GitHub MCP server, falling back to gh only where that binary exists. Returns the PR URL or a Blocked report. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/agents/away-team-pr-writer.md) · 3 copies)
- **away-team-reviewer**: Works through the open review threads on a pull request. Applies the small, local ones (nit, rename, one-function change, missing test) with a commit each, replies on every thread, pushes only when the user confirmed, and leaves larger design questions to the author with a proposal. Returns a Revision report or a Blocked report. ([scotscottmca/away-team](https://github.com/scotscottmca/away-team/blob/HEAD/agents/away-team-reviewer.agent.md) · 3 copies)

- **awf-lookup**: awf 查詢員：用 awf 的唯讀子指令與讀檔把資料查出來、整理好再回報，主對話只拿結論。使用者問「這個 run 卡在哪／為什麼停了／下一步要下什麼」（診斷）、「核准前幫我整理重點／這一段要我決定什麼」（閘門摘要）、「錢花在哪／哪個角色最花 token」（成本）、「交付文件哪些過期、還缺什麼」（交付盤點）、「設定有沒有問題／doctor 的警告是什麼意思」（設定體檢），或「某個需求在文件與程式的哪裡、改了會影響什麼」「把相關的規格、決定、紀錄整理成摘要」時主動派它。只讀：不改檔、不核准、不推送、不跑 workflow。 ([s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow](https://github.com/s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/awf-lookup.md) · ★1)
- **awf-refresh**: awf 更新員：把專案的衍生資料更新到最新——重建知識庫索引（awf kb build）、更新知識圖（awf kg build）、重寫交付文件總目錄（awf deliverables index），並回報更新了什麼、還有哪些要人處理。適合「文件改了很多，索引要重建」「知識圖舊了」「交付文件的目錄沒跟上」這類耗時、輸出很長、主對話只需要結果的工作。只能跑這幾個固定的更新指令，其餘唯讀：不改專案的檔、不核准、不推送、不跑 workflow。 ([s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow](https://github.com/s96cax98leo/AgentWorkflow/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/awf-refresh.md) · ★1)

Writes deliverables/05-architecture/cloud/aws*.md. / Especialista en AWS de Modernia: arquitectura, migración y costos en Amazon Web Services. ([gvaldesmty1/modernia](https://github.com/gvaldesmty1/modernia/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/aws-specialist.md))
- **aws-specialist**: Fallback AWS assistant. Use for any AWS documentation lookup, CLI command, service explanation, or architecture question that does not match a more specific agent (aws-waf-reviewer, aws-cost-analyzer, aws-security-auditor). ([smsilva/linux](https://github.com/smsilva/linux/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/aws-specialist.md))
- **aws-ssm-deployer**: AWS deployment and log investigation agent — discovers EC2 instances by tag, deploys via SSM (no SSH), monitors health via Cloud Map, streams CloudWatch logs, and investigates gossip/quorum anomalies. Merges cloud-deploy + log-investigator. ([Vishal-jain2003/EDgefabric-real](https://github.com/Vishal-jain2003/EDgefabric-real/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aws-ssm-deployer.md))
- **aws-storage-adapter**: Use when working on the S3 artifact storage boundary for upload, retrieval, naming policy, lineage evidence, and publish-stage archive interactions. - **aws-strands-harness**: In October 2026, AWS open-sourced **Strands Harness** (The New Stack), demonstrating that agent harness defaults—specifically context management, output bounding, and loop recovery—can reduce token spend by **45% compared to Claude Code and Codex**, and by **77% on Terminal-Bench 2.1 ($56.29 vs $248.05)**. ([IgorGanapolsky/ThumbGate](https://github.com/IgorGanapolsky/ThumbGate/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/aws-strands-harness.md))
- **aws-terraform-writer**: Expert infrastructure-as-code agent specializing in authoring production-grade, enterprise AWS Terraform modules. Dynamically fetches real-time provider schemas via terraform-mcp-server, applies architectural patterns from attached skills, and generates modular, secure HCL code. ([talkops-ai/opscode](https://github.com/talkops-ai/opscode/blob/HEAD/src/opscode/built_in_subagents/aws-terraform-module-writer/agents/aws-terraform-writer.md) · ★3)
- **aws-test-engineer**: Generate, run, and fix comprehensive tests for AWS Python projects. Analyzes actual handler code to produce E2E, integration, contract, performance, and load tests. Works with Lambda, API Gateway, Step Functions, and Batch jobs. ([whitewhiteqq/aws-test-plugin](https://github.com/whitewhiteqq/aws-test-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/aws-test-engineer.md) · ★2)
- **aws-verifier**: Read-only AWS deployment verifier. Polls ECS service events, target group health, and ALB URL to determine stable/unstable state after a deploy. Exits early on definitive failure; requires a 3-minute healthy window before declaring stable. Use after a Terraform apply or ECS deploy to confirm the service actually came up. ([acarmonag/fullstack-devops-agent-aws-terraform](https://github.com/acarmonag/fullstack-devops-agent-aws-terraform/blob/HEAD/codebase/rdicidr-0.1.0/.claude/agents/aws-verifier.md))
- **aws-wa-reviewer**: AWS Well-Architected reviewer. Use for architecture reviews, security assessments, reliability improvements, cost optimization, performance reviews, sustainability checks, and operational excellence audits. Invoked automatically by /wafr command. Examples: "review full architecture", "check security", "ver custos", "reliability", "performance", "sustainability", "ops excellence", "cost". ([Rassis7/my-opencode](https://github.com/Rassis7/my-opencode/blob/HEAD/agents/aws-wa-reviewer.md) · ★1)

Use proactively when a new workload design is described or when assessing production readiness of AWS infrastructure. Triggers: "is this well-architected?", "review my AWS setup", "assess this architecture", "production readiness", "best practices audit". - **ax-ai-native-reviewer**: AX(AI 전환)/AI-native 렌즈. 이게 진짜 AI로 문제를 푼 건지 얇은 래퍼인지, maker→checker 루프 설계와 로그 진정성을 채점한다. 담당 축 = ai_native, honesty_presentation. maker≠checker 신선 심사관. - **ax-browser**: Use this runbook when a coding agent cannot open, operate or capture an isolated local Web Client. Keep three independent results: **Host transport**, **browser navigation**, and **rendered interaction**. A successful result in one does not certify the others. ([BotHarness/DeepSeekBot](https://github.com/BotHarness/DeepSeekBot/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ax-browser.md) · ★932)
- **ax-builder**: Builds agent-facing docs and CLI for EYES: llms.txt, llms-full.txt, openapi.json, agent-card.json, self-signup flow docs, eyes CLI. Use for T3.2 and T3.4. ([ayaangazali/eyes](https://github.com/ayaangazali/eyes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ax-builder.md) · ★2)
- **ax-builder**: Feature-checkpoint builder. Implements one logical slice from a brief — may touch several files when they form one cohesive change (e.g. handler + service + test). Writes TDD: failing test first, then implementation. Refuses cross-cutting/architectural ambiguity — bounces back for a clearer brief. For read-only location use ax-investigator; for review use ax-reviewer. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-builder.md))
- **ax-debug**: Heavyweight hypothesis-driven debugger. OPT-IN, not the default path — dispatch only when a test breaks or the orchestrator is stuck, never as routine implementation. Forms a symptom → hypothesis → cheapest-test chain to root cause, lands a failing repro test, then fixes. The brief sets mode: diagnose-only (read-only) or diagnose+fix. Reports evidence up; the orchestrator owns the final "done" claim. Backs /ax-diagnose. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-debug.md))
- **ax-git-scout**: Read-only scanner for stale git state. Inspects `.worktrees/*`, local branches, and (optionally) remote tracking refs for cleanup candidates: merged into base, gone upstream, stale by age, missing on disk, or dirty. Returns an indexed structured report. Never mutates state. Dispatched by /ax-git-cleanup; the orchestrator confirms before any deletion. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-git-scout.md))
- **ax-haiku**: Lightweight background runner for polling, status checks, log scraping, and structured extraction — work that needs no Sonnet judgment. Dispatched with a self-contained brief: the caller embeds full instructions in the prompt. Read-only by default; the brief sets scope. Use for CI watch, deploy watch, log tail, simple file lookups. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-haiku.md))
- **ax-investigator**: Read-only code locator. Answers "where is X defined", "what calls Y", "list uses of Z", "map this directory". Returns a file:line table, no prose. Refuses to suggest fixes or speculate about design. Use to save main-context tokens on exploration. For edits use ax-builder; for "is this the right approach" use a strategist. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-investigator.md))
- **ax-model**: Use DSH's built-in `opencode-go` route for AX-launched isolated Profiles after a one-time machine-local setup. The default model is `deepseek-v4.1-flash`; the setup script checks the model against the catalog shipped with the worktree's pinned native adapter. This uses no additional Provider plugin. ([BotHarness/DeepSeekBot](https://github.com/BotHarness/DeepSeekBot/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ax-model.md) · ★932)
- **ax-performance**: Performance is a behavior: a change can be correct and still make the app slow, hot or janky. This spec says when an agent must observe and verify performance, what to measure, what counts as evidence, and how a regression is caught before it ships. It exists because idle CPU in Bot mode regressed unnoticed until a Human reported it ([#2413](https://github.com/BotHarness/DeepSeekBot/issues/2413)). ([BotHarness/DeepSeekBot](https://github.com/BotHarness/DeepSeekBot/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ax-performance.md) · ★932)
- **ax-plan**: Planner / large-context combiner. Researches online + across the codebase, reasons through large planning problems, and writes the design doc + spec in its OWN context to keep the orchestrator's cache clean. Subsumes evidence-gathering: chases a hunch through primary sources before committing a plan. May summon any subagent EXCEPT ax-builder. Model is chosen per dispatch (Fable 5 | Opus 4.8); the orchestrator passes the model override. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-plan.md))
- **ax-resource-cleanup**: This procedure applies to task-owned verification Hosts, Client/Metro watchers, previews, browser drivers, tunnels and temporary worktrees. Resource cleanup is part of task completion, including after a failed verification, timeout or interrupted run. Shared device resources remain under the [device QA queue](device-qa.md), including its Windows lane. ([BotHarness/DeepSeekBot](https://github.com/BotHarness/DeepSeekBot/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ax-resource-cleanup.md) · ★932)
- **ax-reviewer**: - Role: focused AI/search-audience reviewer, not implementer or bot-access bypass operator. - Input: scope/policy; bounded evidence bundle; tool limits; evidence rules; `references/ai-search-ax.md`; native-app applicability when relevant. Include the coverage/finding field shapes and semantic rules from report-contract.md; the worker does not need its plan schema or validator instructions. - Task: ([luongnv89/skills](https://github.com/luongnv89/skills/blob/HEAD/extras/ux-ax-review/agents/ax-reviewer.md) · ★147)
- **ax-reviewer**: Read-only diff reviewer. Reviews a change against its brief, re-runs the test signals the builder claimed, flags correctness/security/scope problems. One line per finding, severity-tagged. Flag-only — names the problem, not the fix (the orchestrator owns fix-finding). No praise, no scope creep. Ends with `CONFIDENCE: &lt;0–100&gt;` so the orchestrator can aggregate into repo health and decide proceed/fix/block. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-reviewer.md))
- **ax-schema-reviewer**: Reviews ax SurrealDB schema + ingest-stage changes for the registration gotchas that fail CI. ([Necmttn/ax](https://github.com/Necmttn/ax/blob/HEAD/.ax/agents/ax-schema-reviewer.md) · ★123)
- **ax-signals-inferrer**: Full signals pipeline: runs `apex signals scan` for the deterministic substrate, infers domain structure, authors `.claude/project/signals.md`, and wires the @-ref into CLAUDE.md. Scoped writes ONLY — never touches files outside `.claude/project/` and the @-ref target. Dispatched by /ax-refresh-signals (interactive) and ship verbs (silent). On large repos may dispatch ax-investigator per domain. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-signals-inferrer.md))
- **ax-strategist**: Read-only heavyweight reasoning. Audits plans, specs, and designs; reasons through hard tradeoffs; surfaces hidden assumptions. Answers "is this the right approach?" — not "is this code correct?" (that's ax-reviewer) and not "where is X?" (that's ax-investigator). Does not implement, locate, or gate diffs. Reports findings up; the orchestrator decides. ([FNGApex/apex-claude](https://github.com/FNGApex/apex-claude/blob/HEAD/example/agents/ax-strategist.md))
- **ax-verifier**: Independent EYES agent-experience verifier for MCP, REST, docs and CLI PRs and every gate. ([ayaangazali/eyes](https://github.com/ayaangazali/eyes/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ax-verifier.md) · ★2)
- **ax-verifier**: Adversarial verification of a desktop automation outcome. Proves the action landed — or proves it did not. ([HARSHITJAIS14/llmos](https://github.com/HARSHITJAIS14/llmos/blob/HEAD/atree/agents/ax-verifier.md))
- **ax-writer**: Prose drafter for human-facing markdown — README, docs/guides, CHANGELOG narrative. Inverts the terse Apex output style: clear, expansive, detailed narrative.
